Now, in tabs layout with the Full tab active, every filter-creation path — filter popup (create *and* edit-save), right-click "after/before this", AND/OR bulk combine, link dialog, time-context dialog, filter load — switches to the Filtered tab; the Stacked layout is deliberately left untouched (both panels already visible). The same reveal fixed a real bug in `jumpToEntry()` (bookmark jump) and `jumpToFullLog()`: their scroll+flash targets the Filter view, so a bookmark clicked while the Full tab was active scrolled an invisible panel. The invert toggle intentionally does *not* jump — it doesn't activate the toggled node, so flipping the tab onto a possibly unrelated active filter would be surprising.
  2. **Bookmark toggle repaints both views immediately**: the `B` shortcut previously repainted nothing (the dot appeared only on the next unrelated render), and the row-context-menu path repainted only the Filter view, leaving the Full view's dot stale. `toggleBookmark()` now re-renders both visible row windows itself (safe for double-click detection — neither trigger is the first half of a row double-click).
  3. **Minimap background buckets memoized** (`minimapBgCache`) — see Known limitations (resolved entry) for details; removes an O(root file) pass from every node/filter switch.
  4. **Scoped cache invalidation on tail ticks** (`invalidateCachesForRoots`) — see Memoization; filters under untouched files no longer recompute every 1.5s while another file is being tailed.
  5. **Per-node level-count cache** (`node._levelCounts` / `getLevelCounts()`) — see Memoization; removes an O(active view) `levelBucket` pass from every render when flipping between already-computed filters.
  6. **`computeHighlightMap` runs once per `renderMainView()`** instead of twice — `renderHighlightView(colorMapIsFresh)` skips its own recompute when the caller just built the map; the other caller (`revealInHighlightView`) still recomputes.
  7. **Arrow-key navigation halved its per-keypress scan**: `selectEntry` accepts an `opts.index` hint, passed by `moveSelection` and the minimap click, so a large view isn't `findIndex`-scanned twice per keypress.

- Three follow-ups to the Stacked layout: (1) **Reordered to Full-on-top / Filtered-on-bottom** — an actual DOM reorder inside `#fhSplit`, not a CSS `order` hack, so `#fhSplitResizer`'s drag math also flipped sign to match (dragging down now shrinks `#filterSlot`, the bottom panel, same convention as `#detailResizer`). (2) **Level quick-filter now updates both views immediately** — the filtering logic was already shared (`state.levelFilter`/`applyLevelFilter`), but the toggle-button click handler only re-rendered the Filtered view; it now calls `renderMainView()` instead of `renderTable()`, so the Full view picks up the new level selection in the same click instead of lagging until an unrelated render. (3) **Per-panel "Full"/"Filtered" identifier badges** (`.fh-panel-badge`, top-right corner of each panel) — shown only in Stacked layout, where there's no active-tab-button highlighting to say which panel is which at a glance. See "Highlight view (Filter/Highlight split)".
- Five changes to the Filtered/Full split and the tree: (1) **Resizable sidebar width** (`#sidebarResizer`, between the tree and the content area) and **resizable Stacked-layout split** (`#fhSplitResizer`, between the Filtered and Full panels when the view toggle is set to "Stacked") — both drag handles built the same way as the existing detail-panel resizer, session-only (not persisted to `localStorage`), same as it. (2) **Edit a filter** via `F2` or right-click → "Edit filter…" — reopens the filter popup pre-filled with the node's current value, updates it in place instead of creating a new child (`updateFilterNode`); only for `text`/`extract` filters, the two types with a plain editable value. (3) **Highlight-rule markers now also render in the Filtered view** (`renderVisibleRows`), not just the Full view — `highlightColorMap` is computed once per `renderMainView()` call, before either view renders, so both are correct on first paint. (4) The Full view's UI label was **"Highlight" → "Full"** — internal identifiers (`renderHighlightView`, `#highlightWrap`, etc.) intentionally left as-is, see "Highlight view (Filter/Highlight split)". (5) The Filter/Highlight tab switcher and the separate stacked-layout button were **merged into one tri-state toggle** (`#fhTabs`: Full / Filtered / Stacked, `applyFhView`) — replaces `#btnFhLayout` entirely. See "Highlight view (Filter/Highlight split)" and "Tree interactions".
- Bugfix: Filter view (plain table and, by extension, the tab bar/split around it) rendered with the header and rows side by side instead of stacked, in both tabs and stacked layout. Root cause: the `#fhSplit`/Highlight-view CSS edit that introduced the Filter/Highlight split (see below) accidentally dropped the base `#tableWrap{display:flex; flex-direction:column; min-height:0; position:relative;}` rule during a cleanup pass. JS still sets `tableWrap.style.display = "flex"` inline (unchanged), so `#tableWrap` was still a flex container — just with no `flex-direction` set, meaning it fell back to the browser default `row` instead of `column`, laying `#tableHeader` and `#tableBody` next to each other instead of one above the other. `#highlightWrap` was unaffected (it gets its flex-column layout directly from the `.fh-panel` class, not from a same-shaped sibling rule), which is why only the Filter view showed the bug. Fixed by restoring the original rule verbatim.
- Highlight view: a second, always-full-file view (`#highlightWrap`) alongside the existing Filter view (`#tableWrap`/`#linkWrap`, now `#filterSlot`), toggled tabs/stacked via `#fhTabBar`/`#btnFhLayout` (persisted like the theme). Any filter-type tree node (not file, not `extract`) can get a highlight colour via a new swatch button — replacing the old always-visible multi-select checkbox there, multi-select is Ctrl+click-only now — opened through a custom hue-wheel + preset colour picker (`#colorPickerPopup`, deliberately not the native `<input type="color">`). Matches for every colored node in the tree are computed on each Highlight-view render (`computeHighlightMap`) and shown as small edge-stripe markers (`.hl-marker`), several per row if multiple rules match — no color priority/blending. Double-click in the Filter view or link view now reveals+selects the entry in the Highlight view (`revealInHighlightView`) instead of the old destructive full-log jump; the extract table's double-click keeps the old `jumpToFullLog` behavior since it has no Highlight companion. `state.selectedId` (already shared) keeps both views' selection in sync; the Highlight view has its own scroll/virtualization track that (unlike the Filter view) doesn't reset position on every render. `highlightColor` persists through filter save/load JSON, copy/paste, and undo/redo alongside the node's other fields. See "Highlight view (Filter/Highlight split)".

- Undo/redo (`Ctrl+Z` / `Ctrl+Shift+Z` / `Ctrl+Y`, toolbar buttons) for filter node delete and move — snapshot-based stack (`snapshotSubtree`/`restoreSubtree`, ids preserved so external `linkedId` references self-heal on restore), routed through two wrapped mutators (`deleteFilterNodeWithUndo`/`moveFilterNodeWithUndo`) that every delete/move trigger (Delete key, row ✕, tree context menu, drag/drop, Ctrl+X paste) now goes through. Deliberately narrow v1 scope — not files, not value edits, not toggles. See "Undo / redo (filter-tree delete & move)".
- Bookmarks: right-click a row → bookmark it, or press `B` with a row selected; toolbar button + count badge opens a panel (sorted by time, editable per-bookmark note, click to jump). Session-only (not saved to filter JSON), scoped to real log entries only. New `jumpToEntry()` generalizes the existing `jumpToFullLog` to work from anywhere, not just the currently active file's chain. See "Bookmarks".
- Column statistics: min/max/mean/stddev per numeric extraction column, shown directly above the table (`#extractStatsBar`) — no round-trip through Excel needed for a quick plausibility check. See "Column statistics".
- Value assertions on extraction columns: per-column range (min/max) or target±tolerance check, set via a small dialog behind a target-icon button in each numeric column header. Violations tint the table cell and show a "N of M outside range" header badge; the Plot tab draws an extra error-colored ring on violating points. Persisted through filter save/load and copy/paste alongside the node's other fields. See "Value assertions (extraction columns)".
- Timeline minimap Start/End/Duration line (`#timelineMinimapMeta`, above the density bars): plain-text summary of the root file's time span — `formatTime()` for the two timestamps, the new sign-free `formatMs()` (factored out of `formatDelta`, now with a day-scale branch) for the duration. Recomputed on every `renderTimelineMinimap()` call, so it keeps growing live while a tailed file is being followed. Hidden on the mobile breakpoint to keep the strip's footprint small there. Width is measured off the `#timelineMinimap` container (unaffected by the meta line, which sits in its own row and consumes no horizontal space) and height off the SVG itself (the container's clientHeight now includes the meta line, which would under-report the bars' actual height) — measuring both off the container was tried first and visibly narrowed the bars in real browsers (some SVG-replaced-element clientWidth quirk under the new flex-column layout, not fully root-caused), so `preserveAspectRatio="none"` was also added to the SVG as a standing guard against that whole class of viewBox/box-size mismatch.
- Timeline minimap (`#timelineMinimap`, above the status strip, plain log view only — hidden for extract/link views like the status strip): SVG density strip spanning the active node's root file. Grey background bars are whole-file density; colored overlay bars are the current filtered/leveled view, one worst-level color per bucket (via `LEVEL_SORT_RANK`) so a lone ERROR isn't lost among DEBUG noise; both use a shared log scale (`renderTimelineMinimap`) so overlay bars never exceed their background bar for the same bucket. A translucent rect tracks the table's scrolled time range (`updateMinimapViewportIndicator`, repositioned on every scroll frame rather than rebuilding the whole SVG). Click jumps to the nearest entry via `lowerBoundByTs` + the existing `selectEntry({scroll:true})` path. Bucketing is always by `entry.ts`, so the density bars stay meaningful under a column sort — only click-to-jump and the viewport rect assume row order = time order, so those two are withheld (`minimapInteractive`, `.no-jump` class) while `state.sortColumn` is set; the bars themselves are not. Hover tooltip reads the same per-bucket count/rank arrays the render pass built. Rebuilds on node/filter switch (called from `renderTable`) and on window resize; the viewport rect alone updates on scroll.
- Δt column (`.col-delta`, between Time and Level): time since the previous row *in the order shown*, via `formatDelta()` (`+123ms` / `+12.3s` / `+12m 34s`). Colored via `DELTA_WARN_MS`/`DELTA_ERROR_MS` thresholds (1s / 5s default) for at-a-glance stall/timeout spotting. Only computed when rows are in natural chronological order (`!state.sortColumn`) — dashed out under a column sort, same reasoning as the context-filter bracket suppression, since adjacent rows are no longer adjacent moments once sorted by e.g. Level or Thread.
- Filter save/load (`.json`, right-click → "Save filter…" / "Load filter…"): saves the ancestor chain + subtree of the clicked filter; `and`/`or`/`link` targets outside that branch are pulled in via their own ancestor chain (not dropped) so combined filters always survive intact, even across files — see "Filter save / load" section above. Reload always re-evaluates against the destination tree's own data rather than replaying a stored result; no dedup against an already-present equivalent branch (known gap, see above).
- Bug-fix pass (3 fixes, all regression-tested via jsdom):
  1. Escape with an active extraction-table cell selection no longer throws — the handler called a `setTableSelection()` helper that no longer exists; it now clears `state.tableSelection` directly and refreshes the cell classes (`applyExtractSelectionClasses`). The throw had also been silently preventing the multi-select reset further down in the same handler.
  2. Deleting a file no longer breaks identity lookups in files that share its entry objects: merged files reuse source entries *by reference*, but `deleteNode` unconditionally removed them from `entryIndex`, so deleting a merged file emptied the detail panel for its source files (and vice versa). New helper `releaseEntriesFromIndex(entries, excludeRootId)` only drops ids that no other surviving root file still references, and also clears a now-dangling `state.selectedId`.
  3. Tail rotation/truncation no longer leaks the replaced entries into `entryIndex` for the session lifetime — the rotation branch in `tailTick` now releases them through the same helper (which correctly keeps ids alive if a merged file still holds them).
- Time context filter (`context`): windows the whole root file to everything within N ms before/and-or-after each reference entry, merging overlapping reference windows into single groups (`mergeContextRanges`); result is real entries (not synthetic), so filters chain underneath it normally; reference rows marked with an accent dot, groups shown as a left-side bracket in the log view (suppressed under column sort); created via right-click a filter → "Time context…" (`#contextDialog`, ms before/after); no `linkedId` — reads the root file directly, so none of link/and/or's cycle-guard or global-cache-invalidation concerns apply to it. Excluded from NOT (see Inversion (NOT)) for the same "always contains itself" reason.
- Filter inversion (NOT): generic `inverted` flag on any filter node (set difference against the parent's result in `getEntries()`); NOT checkbox in the filter popup at creation, "Invert (NOT)" in the tree right-click menu after the fact; excluded for `link`/`extract` where the generic definition doesn't hold; `¬` label prefix + red type tag in the tree
- File-drop overlay (`#dropOverlay`) no longer triggers while dragging a filter row in the tree — gated by `isFileDrag()` (`dataTransfer.types.includes("Files")`) so it only reacts to a real OS file drag, not the tree's own internal drag-and-drop
- `and`/`or`/`link` filter nodes can now be copied, cut, and dragged like any other filter — `cloneSubtree()` carries `linkedId` (+ `linkDirection`/`linkN` for `link`) onto the clone instead of dropping it; a narrow cycle guard (`wouldCreateLinkedCycle`/`subtreeHasLinkedCycle`) blocks only the one genuinely unsafe placement (linkedId target becomes an ancestor); a blocked cut keeps the clipboard active instead of silently clearing it
- Tree: right-click a single node (outside an existing multi-selection) for a Copy/Cut/Paste/Delete menu — mouse-only equivalent of the keyboard shortcuts, same underlying functions
- Tree: drag-and-drop reparenting for filter nodes — any node (file or filter) is a valid drop target, reuses `moveNode()`
- Fixed double-click jump-to-full-log, which had silently stopped working entirely: a plain row click no longer tears down and rebuilds every row's DOM node (was defeating the browser's native double-click detection); selection highlight now toggles in place instead
- Removed the message hover popover — redundant with the detail panel, and was the proximate trigger for noticing the double-click bug above (see Testing approach)
- Tailing: files opened via handle (picker/drop, Chromium) update live; auto-follow to newest entry, disengaged by scrolling/row click, re-engaged via floating "Newest" button; live dot in tree; filtered views update live too
- Light theme (`data-theme="light"` toggle in toolbar, persisted to `localStorage`, defaults to `prefers-color-scheme`)
- Log table: sortable Time/Level/Thread/Location columns, always-visible status strip ("N of M shown · filtered to..."), custom selectable hover popover replacing the native title tooltip on message cells, wider severity bar (3px → 5px)
- Extraction workflow: live match-count feedback while typing a filter/pattern, clickable placeholder token chips, per-column sort buttons in the extraction table
- Tree: always-visible per-row checkboxes (primary multi-select method, Ctrl+click still works), type tags (`FILE`/`TXT`/`TIME`/`TBL`/`AND`/`OR`/`LNK`), per-row percentage-of-parent bar, always-visible bulk-action bar above the tree (mirrors the right-click menu via shared `describeBulkActions`/`performBulkAction`)
- Value-extraction plotting (line/bar/scatter, color-by-value, auto/manual axis scaling, per-series normalize)
- Link filter (nearest-neighbor pairing) + paired-results view; extraction over pairs via the `⟶` separator
- Tree multi-select (Ctrl+click) + right-click bulk actions: file merge, AND/OR filter combination
- Right-click "extract numbers from this message" pattern suggestion
- Extraction table: multi-column Ctrl+click selection, pattern↔column hover linking with numbered/colored chips
- Value-extraction filter type (`[value:float]` etc. + `[*]` wildcard) with its own spreadsheet-style view and copy-to-Excel
- Resizable detail panel (full selected message, drag handle)
- Copy vs. cut semantics for filter nodes (Ctrl+C duplicates and stays pasteable multiple times; Ctrl+X is one-time move)
- Row selection (click + arrow keys) with jump-to-full-log (double-click, flash highlight)
- Performance pass: virtualized log view, memoized filter results
- Level quick-filter buttons, per-level row tinting
- Loading progress bar (chunked async parse so the UI doesn't block)
- Initial build: tree-structured filter chain, drag/drop file loading, log4net-pattern parser
